| wp-bound-computer-away-zh | zh | pass | judge: The transcript shows no visible partner message after the failed 'bench' task, and no opened task is listed, so nothing ran on the Mac, the user was not asked anything, and no false claim about hel was made. Silence is allowed by the rubric. |
| wp-no-copy-no-computer-en | en | pass | judge: The partner opened one task on hel with the repo cloned there first, and asked no question about where to clone or which computer. The ack is one short English sentence naming the action and the clone line is the permitted Ducter line. It does not claim the typo is fixed. |
| wp-named-computer-ends-at-once-zh | zh | pass | judge: The work restarts on hel with Codex in the same repo, as a local place on hel rather than the Mac. The user is asked nothing and hears one short Chinese ack line that paraphrases the quota problem without pasting the raw error. The reason clause is slightly more than needed but not a rule violation. |
| wp-other-task-names-owned-pr-zh | zh | pass | judge: After the review task reported the playwright e2e failure on #10932, the partner shows no action: no new task, no question to the user, and no fix request to the review task. The rubric explicitly allows silence, since the copy task already owns the PR and Ducter passes the report along. |
| wp-task-wait-ended-ask-zh | zh | **fail** | judge: The reply correctly says the task is still working and reports the last readable CI look (2 of 3 done), with no promise of a result. But it states as fact that the third check is still running, which nobody could read, so it should have said that status is not known yet. That makes it a dishonest claim about CI, so it fails. |
| wp-long-round-ci-zh | zh | pass | judge: The only step shown is the loop keeper handing its facts over, and no message, card, or ack appears in the transcript. Silence is a valid output here, and nothing asks the user, restarts the task, implies it is stuck, or claims CI passed. |
| wp-long-round-stuck-en | en | pass | judge: The partner tells the user in one short English line what it saw (GitHub sign-in on hel rejected since about 22:00, so CI can't be read) and that renewing the sign-in is the user's fix. It also sends the task a clear next step: stop retrying and report that the sign-in needs renewing. No raw error text is pasted and nothing unsupported is claimed. |

But it runs to five sentences against the rubric's limit of three, and it only says the change request will be handed to the task at the end of this turn, so the task doesn't have it yet and no task_send nudge was sent. |
| wp-partner-meant-for-other-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner forwarded the request to 小家 with the tool call and sent one short Chinese line saying it went to 小家, with no reminder or task set here, no question asked, and no claim that 小家 has already acted. It uses 你 as required. |

The partner created it immediately without asking questions or opening a task, and the reply is a short Chinese line saying it's made, what it manages (家里的事), and how to talk to it (the small stone beside the name). The reply uses 你 and no headings or filler, matching the rubric's requirements. |

## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 你好，我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我去查看某个仓库里挂掉的测试，把它修好，并在你确认后交给你。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can get a broken test in one of your repos fixed on its own branch and tell you what changed.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 코드 수정, 리포지토리 작업, 웹 확인, 메일이나 일정 같은 계정 정리까지 맡길 수 있어요. 오늘 하고 싶은 일이 있으면 편하게 말해 주세요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 봐요. 편하게 쉬어요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証する通信、UDPは速さを優先して届く保証をしない通信です。

TCPは届いたか確認し、順番どおりに再送して渡すので、Webやメールなど確実性が必要な場面で使われます。UDPは再送や順序の管理をせず軽く送るので、動画配信やオンラインゲームのようにリアルタイム性が大事な場面で使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner al día tu rama privada sobre la base antes de compartirla, y así dejas un historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar el registro real de cuándo se integró; nunca hagas rebase de una rama que otros ya están usando.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠的传输：先建立连接，保证数据不丢、不乱、按序到达；UDP 则是无连接、尽力而为，速度快、开销小，但不保证送达。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours and unpushed, so you can tidy your commits and keep history linear before sharing it. Merge when others already have the branch, because rebasing rewrites commits they may have based work on.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database before that change is applied to the data files. If the system crashes midway, it replays the log on restart so committed work survives and half-finished work can be undone.
```

<script>
(function(){
  var $ = function(id){ return document.getElementById(id); };
  var feeds = [];
  var editing = null;

  function pad(n){ return String(n).padStart(2,'0'); }
  function nowHM(){ var d = new Date(); return pad(d.getHours()) + ':' + pad(d.getMinutes()); }
  function today(){ va … 还没喝');
  }

  function load(){
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(function(v){
      feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  }

  $('save').onclick = function(){
    var raw = $('t').value.trim();
    var m = raw.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);
    var ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
    if (!m || !(ml > 0)) return;
    var t = pad(m[1]) + ':' + m[2];
    if (editing){
      editing.t = t; editing.ml = ml;
    } else {
      feeds.push({ id: String(Date.now()), date: today(), t: t, ml: ml });
    }
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    reset();
    render();
  };

  $('del').onclick = function(){
    if (!editing) return;
    var id = editing.id;
    feeds = feeds.filter(function(f){ return f.id !== id; });
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    reset();
    render();
  };

  $('list').onclick = function(e){
    var b = e.target.closest('[data-id]');
    if (!b) return;
    editing = feeds.find(function(f){ return f.id === b.dataset.id; });
    if (!editing) return;
    $('t').value = editing.t;
    $('ml').value = editing.ml;
    $('save').textContent = '保存修改';
    $('del').hidden = false;
  };

  reset();
  load();
  ducter.onChange(load);
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":"1763934600000","date":"2026-11-24","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":"1763944500000","date":"2026-11-24","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### reply-older-task-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让 TTL 那个任务再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话：「这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻」

在现有的 TTL 测试基础上，再加一条测试，覆盖条目正好到期的那一刻（假时钟推进到恰好等于 TTL 的时间点）：
- 到期时刻本身：条目应视为已过期（或按现有约定处理，但需要明确断言并与实现一致）。
- 到期前一刻（比如少 1 毫秒）：条目仍然有效。
- 继续使用假时钟，不要依赖真实计时。

完成标准：新测试通过，并把测试输出（通过数）和新增的断言内容报告回来。在分支 ducter/foo-ttl 上提交，不要合并，不要推送，不要开 PR。
范围外：不改动其他测试，不改动 TTL 的实现逻辑；如果发现实现在到期边界上的行为有问题，只报告，不擅自修改。
地点：~/dev/foo（分支 ducter/foo-ttl）。
```
